backend/.agents/skills/prisma-compute/SKILL.md:101:- `verify-prisma-vs-platform-cli` - Do not assume `prisma app deploy` exists in the ORM CLI; check whether the task should use `@prisma/cli`.
backend/.agents/skills/prisma-compute/SKILL.md:104:- `verify-config-support` - Treat `prisma.compute.ts` as the typed Compute config; inspect the project's config and generated scripts before editing or deploying.
backend/.agents/skills/prisma-compute/SKILL.md:105:- `verify-auth-workspace-support` - Use `@prisma/cli auth workspace` commands for local workspace list/use/logout flows.
backend/.agents/skills/prisma-compute/SKILL.md:109:- `auth-source-precedence` - A non-empty `PRISMA_SERVICE_TOKEN` is the active auth source for commands and local OAuth workspaces are ignored for execution. If it is set but empty, the CLI should fail instead of falling back to stored OAuth.
backend/.agents/skills/prisma-compute/SKILL.md:115:- `auth-service-token-switching` - While `PRISMA_SERVICE_TOKEN` is set, `auth workspace use` is unavailable because the service token is the active auth source; unset the env var to switch local OAuth workspaces. Workspace logout still only cleans local OAuth state.
backend/.agents/skills/prisma-compute/SKILL.md:116:- `auth-storage-awareness` - Local OAuth credentials live in the platform auth file, with workspace metadata in a sidecar context file. Project pins live in `.prisma/local.json`, and CLI app/project state lives in `.prisma/cli/state.json` near `prisma.compute.ts` when present.
backend/.agents/skills/prisma-compute/SKILL.md:120:- `framework-cli-first` - Evaluate deploy readiness against `@prisma/cli app deploy`, not against what `create-prisma` can scaffold.
backend/.agents/skills/prisma-compute/SKILL.md:122:- `framework-create-prisma-defaults-only` - `create-prisma` can provide generated defaults and `compute:deploy`, but it is not the general deploy surface for existing apps.
backend/.agents/skills/prisma-compute/SKILL.md:133:- `config-optional-simple-app` - `prisma.compute.ts` is not required to deploy a normal single app; use flags when there is no durable config.
backend/.agents/skills/prisma-compute/SKILL.md:134:- `config-init-formalizer` - Generate a fresh config with `bunx @prisma/cli@latest init`: it detects the framework, pins name/framework/httpPort (plus entry for Bun/Hono), and offers the Project link. `--format json` writes a dependency-free `prisma.compute.json` instead. `init` refuses when any config already exists, never scaffolds code, and never deploys.
backend/.agents/skills/prisma-compute/SKILL.md:135:- `config-use-prisma-compute-ts` - Put reusable deploy defaults in `prisma.compute.ts` with `defineComputeConfig`, not in `prisma.config.ts`.
backend/.agents/skills/prisma-compute/SKILL.md:137:- `config-monorepo-roots` - For monorepos, use `prisma.compute.ts` to declare app targets, roots, framework defaults, entrypoints, ports, and env inputs.
backend/.agents/skills/prisma-compute/SKILL.md:138:- `config-targets` - In multi-app configs, `@prisma/cli app deploy web` selects the `apps.web` target. Without `[app]`, commands can infer the target from the current directory; otherwise deploy can run all targets while build/run require one.
backend/.agents/skills/prisma-compute/SKILL.md:141:- `config-no-project-branch-secrets` - Do not commit Workspace, Project, Branch, production intent, service tokens, or secret values in `prisma.compute.ts`; keep those in flags, `.prisma/local.json`, env storage, or CI secrets. App-level defaults such as `region`, `root`, `framework`, `entry`, `httpPort`, and non-secret env file paths belong in config.
backend/.agents/skills/prisma-compute/SKILL.md:147:- `env-deploy-loads-dotenv` - Generated deploy scripts may load env via `prisma.compute.ts` or `--env .env`; inspect the actual script/config before redeploy.
backend/.agents/skills/prisma-compute/SKILL.md:148:- `env-migrations-separate` - Redeploy scripts do not run migrations or seed data. Run the appropriate Prisma database scripts separately.
backend/.agents/skills/prisma-compute/SKILL.md:149:- `env-cli-token-name` - `@prisma/cli` uses `PRISMA_SERVICE_TOKEN` for service-token auth.
backend/.agents/skills/prisma-compute/SKILL.md:159:- `deploy-build-logs` - Use `@prisma/cli build logs <build-id>` for GitHub/Console build output. Use `app logs` for runtime deployment logs; the two ids are different.
backend/.agents/skills/prisma-compute/SKILL.md:162:- `deploy-create-project` - Use `--create-project <name>` only when the user wants deploy to create and link a new project; it conflicts with `--project` and `PRISMA_PROJECT_ID`.
backend/.agents/skills/prisma-compute/SKILL.md:163:- `deploy-ops-targets` - App show/open/logs/list-deploys/promote/rollback/remove and domain commands can also accept `[app]` targets from `prisma.compute.ts`.
backend/.agents/skills/prisma-compute/SKILL.md:168:- `sdk-use-cli-first` - Prefer `@prisma/cli app deploy` for app workflows; use `create-prisma` only to scaffold a new app unless the user is building lower-level automation.
backend/.agents/skills/prisma-compute/SKILL.md:169:- `sdk-result-handling` - `@prisma/compute-sdk` returns `Result` values; check `isOk()`/`isErr()` instead of relying on exceptions.
backend/.agents/skills/prisma-compute/SKILL.md:174:1. Inspect the project: package manager, template/framework, `package.json` scripts, Prisma version, Prisma client location, `prisma.compute.ts`, and existing `compute:deploy`.
backend/.agents/skills/prisma-compute/SKILL.md:178:   - existing app deploy: config-backed target when present, generated `compute:deploy`, or `@prisma/cli app build/run/deploy` flags
backend/.agents/skills/prisma-compute/SKILL.md:179:   - new app scaffold: `create-prisma`, then generated `compute:deploy` or `@prisma/cli app deploy`
backend/.agents/skills/prisma-compute/SKILL.md:180:   - low-level automation: `@prisma/compute-sdk` or Management API
backend/.agents/skills/prisma-compute/SKILL.md:184:8. For GitHub/Console builds, inspect the `Prisma Compute Deploy` check run or `build logs <build-id>` before guessing why a build failed.

backend/.agents/skills/prisma-compute/references/compute-config.md:1:# Prisma Compute Config
backend/.agents/skills/prisma-compute/references/compute-config.md:3:Use this reference when creating or updating `prisma.compute.ts`, especially for monorepos, multi-app deploys, reusable framework defaults, env inputs, ports, entrypoints, or build settings.
backend/.agents/skills/prisma-compute/references/compute-config.md:5:`prisma.compute.ts` is not required for every deploy. A simple app can deploy with `@prisma/cli app deploy --framework ... --entry ... --http-port ... --env ...`. The config file exists to make those app-level defaults typed and repeatable.
backend/.agents/skills/prisma-compute/references/compute-config.md:7:For monorepos or multi-app repositories, use `prisma.compute.ts`: it is the practical way to tell Compute which app target lives at which `root` and which framework/entry/env defaults belong to each target.
backend/.agents/skills/prisma-compute/references/compute-config.md:11:Prefer `bunx @prisma/cli@latest init` over hand-writing a fresh single-app config. It detects the framework from the same registry deploy uses, pins `name`, `framework`, and `httpPort` (plus `entry` for Bun and Hono), previews every value with its source, offers the `@prisma/compute-sdk` devDependency for editor types, and offers the Project link. Useful flags: `--framework`, `--entry`, `--http-port`, `--name`, `--no-link`, `--json`.
backend/.agents/skills/prisma-compute/references/compute-config.md:13:`--format json` writes a dependency-free static `prisma.compute.json` instead of the TypeScript config; a later explicit `init --format ts` converts it in place when the config needs to become programmatic. `init` fails with `INIT_CONFIG_EXISTS` when any compute config already exists, never scaffolds application code, and never deploys. Multi-app monorepo configs are still written by hand.
backend/.agents/skills/prisma-compute/references/compute-config.md:17:The canonical file is `prisma.compute.ts`. The loader also accepts:

backend/.agents/skills/prisma-compute/references/compute-config.md:24:prisma.compute.json
backend/.agents/skills/prisma-compute/references/compute-config.md:27:`prisma.compute.json` is the static, dependency-free variant of the same config; it is discovered and loaded like the others.
backend/.agents/skills/prisma-compute/references/compute-config.md:33:When a config is discovered, its directory becomes the Compute project directory for local state: `.prisma/local.json` and `.prisma/cli/state.json` live beside that config, not necessarily inside the app root.

backend/.agents/skills/prisma-compute/references/app-deploy-cli.md:85:For non-interactive or CI work, `@prisma/cli` accepts a workspace service token through `PRISMA_SERVICE_TOKEN`. A non-empty service token takes precedence over stored browser-login credentials, so local OAuth workspace switching does not affect command execution while the env var is set. `auth workspace list --json` may still show local OAuth sessions, but they are not switchable until the service-token env var is unset. Verify auth with `auth whoami` and never print the token value.
